Conclusion: 该方法能够有效实现跨网络的因果效应运输，有望改进网络系统中的干预策略，尤其是在社交网络和公共卫生领域。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects under network interference typically assumes that the network used for training and the network used for deployment coincide. In practice, an intervention is run on one population while the question of interest concerns a different population, and the two generally differ in topology, node-covariate composition, and spillover pathways. Transporting a causal effect across networks is therefore a data-fusion problem that no existing algorithm solves. We employ a selection diagram, extended to the network setting so that covariate shift and structural network shift enter as separate selectors, and derive from it a transport formula for the direct, spillover, and total effects in the deployment population. Each formula makes explicit which interventional mechanism is assumed invariant and which observational distribution must be reweighted. We then turn the formulas into TranCE (Transported Causal Effects), a doubly-robust algorithm combining an interventional outcome model, a domain density-ratio correction, and cross-fitted inference. Extensive experiments on two semi-synthetic benchmarks derived from real-world social networks and on a fully real weather-insurance field experiment, where the transported effects are checked against held-out randomized estimates, confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings have the potential to improve intervention strategies in networked systems, particularly in social networks and public health.

</details>


### [234] [Graphical Design of Interpretable Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18936)
*Pietro Barbiero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改编自 Penrose 张量记号的图形化表示法，用于设计可解释 AI 架构；该记法与 PyTorch einsum 代码一一对应，既能全局展示架构，又能精确描述张量操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的架构表示方式各有不足：符号方程无法让人一眼看清整体架构，而概率图模型和流程图又不描述实际的张量操作，导致关键洞察被隐藏、可复现性受限。作者希望提供一种既有全局视角又能精确对应实现代码的形式语言。

Method: 作者基于 Penrose 张量记号，提出一种面向可解释 AI 架构的图形化记号。该记法与 PyTorch 的 einsum 代码一一对应。作者先用该记号描述若干"构造即解释"的架构（概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加性模型、线性模型混合），再用于图解前沿可解释语言模型 Steerling-8B 的关键架构组件。

Result: 该图形化记号成功为多种可解释架构提供全局视图，并将其映射到 einsum 代码。对 Steerling-8B 的图示揭示了其全局架构特性（如 Steerling 是残差模型）、每个操作的几何解释，并可直接翻译为 33 行 PyTorch 代码。

Conclusion: 所提出的 Penrose 风格图形记号弥补了现有表示方式的不足，兼具全局视角与张量级精确性，能够促进可解释 AI 架构的设计、实现与比较。

Abstract: Designing, implementing, and comparing interpretable architectures requires a formal language to represent them. The most common representations fall short in one of two ways. Symbolic equations give no global view of an architecture at a glance. Probabilistic graphical models and flowcharts do not describe actual tensor manipulations, thus hiding key insights and limiting reproducibility. To close this gap, we introduce a graphical notation for designing interpretable AI architectures, adapted from Penrose tensor notation. This graphical notation gives a global view of an architecture and maps one to one onto PyTorch einsum code. We first use this notation to describe architectures that are interpretable by construction, including concept bottlenecks, sparse probes, prototype networks, neural additive models, and mixtures of linear models. We then diagram the key architectural components of Steerling-8B, a frontier interpretable language model. The diagram yields global insights into the architecture (e.g., showing that Steerling is a residual model), a geometric interpretation of each individual operation, and a direct translation into 33 lines of PyTorch code.

</details>


### [235] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Top-K提示范式与大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL，训练得到化学约束一致的语言模型C3LM，在逆合成预测中实现SOTA，并通过集成策略融合LLM与传统模型的互补反应空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成本质上是一对多的任务，但现有单答案评估与基准测试协议难以刻画这种多样性，导致模型难以捕捉多样且合理的反应预测。

Method: 引入Top-K提示作为训练与推理范式；构建约4560万条已验证反应的超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL；在微调中融合基于ChemCensor的约束奖励与新颖性导向奖励，训练C3LM模型；并分析集成了LLM与传统模型互补反应空间的集成式逆合成系统。

Result: C3LM在分布外基准URSA-expert-2026上取得最先进性能；反应唯一性分析表明LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间，支持集成式逆合成系统。

Conclusion: Top-K、合理性感知的训练范式为未来基于LLM的鲁棒合成规划提供了切实可行的新方向。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [236] [Fuzzy Accuracy Compensates for Label Subjectivity in Classification of Skin Tone Using Wearable Photoplethysmography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18969)
*Padmini Krishnadas,Urs Hackstein,Alen Bosnjakovic,Philip J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用光电容积描记（PPG）信号进行皮肤色调（菲茨帕特里克六分类序数标签）分类。由于标签本身存在主观误差，传统准确率仅40-55%；文章提出"模糊准确率"（预测与真实标签相差不超过一类即视为正确），大幅提升性能，证明PPG信号确实携带可辨别的肤色信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤色调分类的典型准确率很低（40-55%），但作者认为主要瓶颈在于标签本身——菲茨帕特里克肤色等级由人工对照色卡主观判定，存在大量小尺度标注误差。因此需要引入"模糊准确率"来更合理地评估肤色能否从PPG信号中被准确预测，从而为"PPG受肤色影响"提供更有说服力的证据。

Method: 采用三类机器学习方法：(1) 在原始PPG信号上使用深度学习（含模糊交叉熵损失函数）或树模型；(2) 对由对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法生成的信号图像表示使用深度学习；(3) 对从信号中提取的手工特征使用机器学习。模糊准确率将"预测类别与标注类别之差不超过1"视为分类正确。

Result: 在原始信号上的树模型准确率达55%、模糊准确率最高96%（最优结果来自含模糊交叉熵的深度学习方法）；基于SPAR图像的深度学习准确率44%、模糊准确率85%；基于PPG特征的机器学习准确率42%、模糊准确率87%。

Conclusion: PPG信号实现肤色分类是可行的，且具有高模糊准确率，表明模型可准确预测出与观察者所选类别相差至多一类的肤色等级。由此得出结论：PPG信号以可辨识的方式受皮肤色调影响。

Abstract: We consider the problem of classification of skin tone using photoplethysmography (PPG) signals with labels of the ordinal six-class Fitzpatrick skin tones. A typical accuracy for this task is a poor 40-55 %. However, the labels are subjectively determined by comparing the skin with a colour chart, and hence contain widespread small-scale inaccuracies. By working with a "fuzzy accuracy", which deems a prediction of skin tone class to be correct if its difference from the labelled class is not greater than one, much higher accuracy is obtained which provides more convincing evidence that skin tone can be accurately predicted from PPG signals. Three machine learning approaches were used, namely deep learning or tree-based approaches on raw PPG signals, deep learning on image representations of the signals generated by the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method, and machine learning on features extracted from the signals. The first method also employed a fuzzy version of the cross entropy loss function, which gave the best results. Tree-based models on raw signals give accuracies up to 55 % and higher fuzzy accuracies up to 96 %, while deep learning models on the SPAR images obtained lower results of 44 % accuracy and 85 % fuzzy accuracy. The machine learning on PPG features gave similar results to the SPAR method with accuracy of 42 % and fuzzy accuracy of 87 %. We have shown that classification of skin tone using PPG signals is possible with high fuzzy accuracy which implies that our modelling approach enables accurate prediction of skin tone class within at most one class of the observer's choice of class, from which we conclude that PPG signals are affected by skin tone in a discernible way.

</details>


### [237] [Monroe: A Molecular Foundation Model for In-Context Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18982)
*Blazej Banaszewski,Andrew W. Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Monroe 是一个新型分子基础模型（MFM），在 PM6 量子化学数据集 8100 万分子上进行大规模预训练，引入立体化学图表示、构象去噪与嵌入去相关损失、改进的多任务学习，并用 TabPFN 做下游上下文预测。它在 Polaris 基准上匹配或超越现有 MFM，在活性悬崖基准上显著提升，且其 PFN 下游策略还能提升 MiniMol 和 CheMeleon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物活性预测受限于数据稀缺，因为药物发现数据集依赖昂贵耗时的湿实验。因此需要能够编码通用化学知识的分子基础模型，在数据受限场景下仍能良好泛化。

Method: 提出 Monroe 分子基础模型，核心创新包括：扩大规模并在 PM6 量子化学数据集的 8100 万分子上预训练；改进立体化学的图表示；改进训练损失（构象去噪与嵌入去相关）；改进多任务学习；下游采用先验数据拟合模型 TabPFN 进行上下文预测。评估采用基于成对比较的框架来衡量统计显著差异，并进行消融与迁移实验。

Result: 在 Polaris 基准上 Monroe 匹配或超过现有 MFM；在活性悬崖基准上相较先前方法取得显著提升。消融与迁移实验表明 PFN 型下游预测器能显著提升 MiniMol 和 CheMeleon，得到新最优变体 MiniMol_PFN 和 CheMeleon_PFN，说明下游适配策略具有通用性。

Conclusion: Monroe 通过大规模预训练、改进的表示与损失、以及 TabPFN 下游预测，在多个基准上达到或超越现有分子基础模型，尤其在活性悬崖任务上取得显著进展，且其下游策略可迁移到其他模型，具有普适性。

Abstract: Bioassay activity prediction is often data-limited because drug-discovery datasets rely on time-consuming and expensive wet-lab experiments for data generation and evaluation. This challenge has inspired recent research into molecular foundation models (MFMs), which aim to encode general-purpose chemical knowledge into molecular representations that generalize well in data-constrained scenarios. This paper presents Monroe, a new MFM with several innovations over the existing state of the art: increased scale allowing pre-training on over 81 million molecules from the PM6 quantum chemistry dataset; improved graph representation of stereochemistry; improved training losses including conformer denoising and embedding decorrelation; improved multi-task learning; and the use of a prior-data-fitted model (TabPFN) for downstream in-context prediction. Our evaluations use a principled pairwise comparison framework that measures statistically significant performance differences. Across established Polaris benchmarks, Monroe matches or exceeds existing MFMs, while on activity cliff benchmarks, designed to assess utility for molecular discovery, it achieves significant improvements over prior methods. Finally, ablation and transfer experiments show that PFN-based downstream predictors also substantially improve two leading existing models, MiniMol and CheMeleon, yielding new state-of-the-art variants we call MiniMol_PFN and CheMeleon_PFN, suggesting that our downstream adaptation strategy generalizes beyond Monroe. Source code is at github.com/blazejba/monroe.

</details>


### [238] [Harness Continual Learning: Continual Adaptation Beyond Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19013)
*Borui Kang,Jinrui Gu,Junhan Lv,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“Harness 持续学习（HCL）”这一新范式，让冻结的基础模型之外的外挂组件（任务接口、经验记忆、能力图谱、自适应路由器）在顺序经验中进化，并通过“守卫式进化”（更新生成与状态提交分离）抑制外挂层面的遗忘，在文本推理、多模态感知与开放世界交互中实现能力累积与故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习以模型为中心，将模型参数视为随顺序经验变化的唯一状态；但现代智能体还通过提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则等外挂（harness）适应环境。这些内容共同影响后续执行，因此即使冻结模型，外挂更新也可能破坏先前可靠行为。这引出一个新问题：如何在不改动模型的情况下让智能体持续改进外挂状态，同时保留已获得的行为？

Method: 作者形式化定义了 Harness 持续学习（HCL）范式，并引入“外挂层面遗忘（harness-level forgetting）”作为衡量指标。实例化四个面向执行的外挂组件：任务接口（Task Interface）、经验记忆（Experience Memory）、能力图谱（Capability Map）与自适应路由器（Adaptive Router）。提出“守卫式外挂进化（guarded harness evolution）”：由持续优化器（Continual Optimizer）根据执行后反馈生成候选外挂，持续评估器（Continual Evaluator）在核对当前改进、历史保留度与有效性之后才提交候选外挂，从而分离更新生成与状态提交。

Result: 在文本推理、多模态感知和开放世界交互三个场景中，方法展现出能力累积与故障恢复能力，在多个设置下相对相应基线取得超过 10% 的增益。组件消融实验评估了各外挂组件的贡献；受控的保留度扫描（retention sweeps）揭示了可测量的外挂层面遗忘，并表明稳定性—可塑性权衡可被显式调节。

Conclusion: 持续学习应从“以模型为中心”扩展为包含外挂状态的视角。通过冻结基础模型并让外挂围绕其进化，并借助守卫式进化（更新生成与提交解耦）控制外挂层面的遗忘，可以在新任务上持续改进的同时保留既有行为，且稳定性—可塑性权衡可被显式调节。

Abstract: Continual learning has largely been model-centric, treating model parameters as the state that changes with sequential experience. Modern agents can also adapt through a harness of prompts, memories, tools, skills, and routing rules. Because these contents jointly shape later execution, a harness update can disrupt previously reliable behavior even when the model is frozen. This raises a new question: how can an agent continually improve its state outside the model while retaining behavior acquired earlier? We formulate Harness Continual Learning (HCL), a new continual learning paradigm in which the harness evolves around a frozen foundation model, and define the resulting loss of earlier behavior as harness-level forgetting. We instantiate HCL with four execution-facing components: the Task Interface, Experience Memory, Capability Map, and Adaptive Router. We further introduce guarded harness evolution to separate update generation from state commitment. A Continual Optimizer proposes candidate harnesses from post-execution feedback, and a Continual Evaluator commits the resulting candidate harness only after checking current improvement, historical retention, and validity. Experiments on textual reasoning, multimodal perception, and open-world interaction demonstrate capability accumulation and failure recovery, with relative gains exceeding 10% over corresponding baselines in multiple settings. Component ablations assess the contribution of each harness component, while controlled retention sweeps reveal measurable harness-level forgetting and show that the stability--plasticity trade-off can be explicitly adjusted.

</details>


### [239] [Multi-Agent Off-Policy Deep Reinforcement Learning for Smart Campus Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19049)
*Omar Rady,Mohamed Ayman,Ali Arafa,Mohamed Shalma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在非凸校园拓扑环境下毫米波基站的最优部署问题，将其建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并系统对比了四种深度强化学习方案，发现多智能体DDPG在密集场景下表现最优，实现全覆盖并取得0.94的Jain公平指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波基站的最优部署问题因max-min公平性目标的非凸、非光滑特性而属于NP-hard问题，传统优化方法难以在真实非凸校园拓扑下高效求解，因此需要借助深度强化学习的实时自适应能力来克服这些限制。

Method: 将基站部署问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），系统性地基准测试四种DRL方案：离散的单智能体深度Q网络（DQN）、空间分区的多智能体DQN、连续的单智能体深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG），以及地理分区的多智能体DDPG框架，并在真实非凸校园拓扑下进行数值评估。

Result: 数值评估表明，多智能体DDPG方法在密集场景下显著优于单智能体方案，实现了全覆盖，并获得0.94的Jain公平指数；此外，多智能体方法在400用户的密集场景下展现出高效的计算收敛性。

Conclusion: 多智能体深度强化学习（尤其是地理分区的多智能体DDPG）是应对非凸、NP-hard毫米波基站部署问题的有效方案，在覆盖率、公平性和计算收敛效率上均优于其他基准方法，适合大规模密集用户场景。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently gained a great attention due to its real-time adaptation and effectiveness in complex optimization problems. This paper investigates the optimal deployment of millimeter-wave (mmWave) base stations (BSs) in a realistic, non-convex campus topology. The optimization problem is NP-hard, due to the non-convex, non-smooth nature of the max-min fairness objective. To overcome these constraints, we formulate the BS placement as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and systematically benchmark four DRL schemes: a discrete single-agent Deep Q-Network (DQN), a spatially partitioned Multi-Agent DQN, a continuous single-agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG), and a geographically partitioned multi-agent DDPG framework. Numerical evaluations reveal that the multi-agent DDPG approach substantially outperforms single-agent in dense scenarios. Additionally full coverage is achieved, and a fairness Jain's index of 0.94 is obtained. Finally, the multi-agent demonstrates highly efficient computational convergence of dense scenarios with $400$ users.

</details>


### [240] [Does Mapping Non-Maximal Probabilities to GMM Components Matter for S-JEPA Encoder Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19084)
*Wenxuan He,Yunpeng Li,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两个对照实验证明：S-JEPA 软目标中非最大概率值如何映射到 GMM 分量，这一"映射信息"（而非仅概率数值本身）对 Encoder 学得的表征有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-JEPA 使用软高斯混合模型（GMM）后验而非硬聚类标签以保留不确定性。但尚不清楚：是否仅概率数值即足以决定所学表征，还是说"哪些 GMM 分量获得非最大概率"这一映射关系也同样重要。

Method: 设计两个匹配对照：FIXED-RANDPERM 保留 top-1 分量及其概率，并保留非最大概率值的多重集合，但按每个物理帧固定的映射重新分配这些非最大概率值；UNIFORM-TAIL 保留 top-1 分量、其概率以及非最大概率的总质量，但将该质量均匀分布。在三个独立随机种子、两种冻结 Encoder 读出器上评估，并在两个暴露实验中以及在线 GMM 切换后的 Phase 2 轨迹上做描述性追踪。

Result: REAL SOFT 在两种冻结 Encoder 读出器上均优于两个对照：更好地恢复原始 GMM 尾部，且在控制当前帧完整频谱后，在短时间尺度上对频谱动态具有更高的可及性。两个暴露实验中，随着更多帧保留原始映射，两种读出器整体均改善。

Conclusion: 软目标的数值概率结构并不能完全决定所学 Encoder 表征；非最大概率到 GMM 分量的映射关系同样至关重要。

Abstract: S-JEPA uses soft Gaussian mixture model (GMM) posteriors instead of hard cluster labels to preserve uncertainty. It remains unclear whether the probability values alone are sufficient, or whether it also matters which GMM components receive the non-maximal probabilities. We test this with two matched controls. FIXED-RANDPERM keeps the top-1 component and probability together with the multiset of non-maximal probability values, but reassigns those non-maximal values using a mapping fixed for each physical frame. UNIFORM-TAIL keeps the top-1 component, its probability, and total non-maximal mass but distributes that mass uniformly. Across three independent seeds, REAL SOFT outperforms both controls on two frozen Encoder readouts. It provides better recovery of the original GMM tail and greater accessibility of spectral dynamics over short time scales after controlling for the complete spectrum of the current frame. In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [241] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多教师在线策略蒸馏（M-OPD）的能力整合问题展开研究，通过构建可控基准揭示其存在严重的能力整合差距（仅恢复35.6%的可提升空间），并诊断出三大失衡根源，进而提出Open-MOPD框架将恢复率提升至83.4%，同时完全开源训练配方与评测套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M-OPD虽在实践中有望将多个领域专精的RL专家整合为单一通才学生，但其优化动力学机制未被充分理解，且缺乏严谨、可复现的公开方案。作者希望弄清多教师能力整合为何存在差距，并提供可复现的研究基础。

Method: 在SmolLM3-3B-Base上构建使用oracle路由的可控M-OPD基准，将能力整合与路由歧义解耦；通过实验诊断识别出三个正交的失败因素：跨领域结构性的序列长度差异、非均匀学习率导致的动态收敛漂移、异步策略更新引起的多步奖励陈旧性；据此提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token份额平衡（token-share balancing）、间隙感知的动态预算分配（gap-aware dynamic budget allocation）和学生奖励刷新（student reward refresh）三种机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅捕获35.6%的可达上限（相对领域路由oracle集成），且简明任务（如指令遵循）严重退化并过早停滞。失败根源不是梯度冲突，而是token级优化预算的严重错配。Open-MOPD将可提升空间的恢复率从35.6%提升至83.4%，在单个可部署学生模型中实现。

Conclusion: 多教师能力整合的瓶颈在于token级优化预算的错配，而非梯度冲突。通过token份额平衡、间隙感知动态预算分配与学生奖励刷新，Open-MOPD系统性地恢复了跨领域平衡，显著提升了能力整合效率，并以学术友好的硬件预算完全开源端到端训练配方、训练轨迹与评测套件。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [242] [Pretraining Reusable Inference Across Views with Synthetic Task Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19115)
*Jielong Lu,Zhihao Wu,Jiajun Yu,Zhaoliang Chen,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多视图学习重新定义为一种可复用、任务条件化的推理过程，并据此设计了 SIMPLE——一个先验拟合的多视图上下文学习者，仅凭少量支持集即可预测查询标签，并借助合成任务先验与分层推理架构，使多视图推理本身可被预训练和复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预训练编码器使异构视图的表征越来越可复用，但决定视图效用并组合证据的过程仍为每个下游任务重新学习，导致关于视图相关性、互补性、可靠性及缺失性的知识被反复丢弃而非跨任务迁移。因此作者主张把多视图学习从学习固定融合函数，转变为学习可复用的、任务条件化的推理过程。

Method: 作者提出 SIMPLE（prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner），通过小规模带标签支持集进行条件化来预测查询标签。由于真实数据集视图配置与任务结构覆盖有限，作者在嵌入空间构造可控的合成任务先验，生成多样化的支持-查询片段（episode），涵盖类结构变化、共享与视图特有因子、表征几何、跨视图依赖、可靠性水平、缺失模式及分布偏移。随后采用分层推理架构，依次在视图内、跨视图以及跨支持与查询样本间进行推理。

Result: 在多视图与多组学基准上的实验表明：SIMPLE 的冻结（frozen）变体在不更新推理主干的情况下即可取得有竞争力的性能；而轻量级适配器校准在多数数据集上达到领先性能。在冻结、单样本（one-shot）及缺失视图设置下的结果共同支持核心假设。

Conclusion: 多视图推理本身可以被预训练并复用，而无需为每个下游任务重新学习融合过程；当需要任务特定对齐时，轻量级适配器校准即可提供所需的适应能力，从而验证了将多视图学习重构为可复用推理过程的观点。

Abstract: Modern pretrained encoders make representations from heterogeneous views increasingly reusable, but the procedure that determines view utility and combines evidence is still relearned for each downstream task. Consequently, knowledge about view relevance, complementarity, reliability, and missingness is repeatedly discarded rather than transferred across tasks. We therefore reformulate multi-view learning as learning a reusable, task-conditioned inference procedure rather than a fixed fusion function. Based on this perspective, we propose SIMPLE, a prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner that predicts query labels by conditioning on a small labeled support set. Since existing real-world datasets cover only a limited range of view configurations and task structures, we construct a controllable synthetic task prior in embedding space. It generates diverse support-query episodes with varying class structures, shared and view-specific factors, representation geometries, cross-view dependencies, reliability levels, missingness patterns, and distribution shifts. A hierarchical inference architecture then performs reasoning within views, across views, and across support and query samples. Experiments on multi-view and multi-omics benchmarks demonstrate that the frozen variant of SIMPLE achieves competitive performance without updating the inference backbone, while lightweight adapter calibration attains leading performance on most evaluated datasets. Together, the results under frozen, one-shot, and missing-view settings support the central hypothesis that multi-view reasoning itself can be pretrained and reused, while lightweight adapter calibration provides task-specific alignment when needed.

</details>


### [243] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成对抗网络的超分辨率框架（SRGAN），用于加速锂离子电池电极材料的EBSD表征，可在降低采集分辨率的同时保持微观结构分析的准确性，实现最高25倍的采集提速或视场扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD是定量表征锂离子电池电极材料微观结构、优化电池性能的关键方法，但其采集速度慢，难以获得具有统计代表性的微观结构数据集，从而限制了高通量分析的开展。

Method: 采用SRGAN（超分辨率生成对抗网络）对LiNixMnyCozO2（NMC）正极颗粒的低分辨率EBSD数据进行计算增强，并与经典插值方法在2x至12x多种放大倍数下进行对比；通过定性图像指标和定量微观结构分析（晶粒尺寸、形状、晶界长度等）评估性能。

Result: SRGAN在所有放大倍数下均系统性地优于经典插值方法，尤其在保留小晶粒和真实晶界方面表现突出。5x放大倍数（对应25倍采集提速或25倍视场扩大）被证明实用可行；在该倍数下，晶粒等面积直径相对误差为+5.7%，晶粒最大内切球直径为+8.2%，晶界长度为-14.6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的SRGAN方法显著提升了EBSD采集效率，可获得更具统计鲁棒性的微观结构数据集，使EBSD成为材料和工业过程开发中的高通量表征工具。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [244] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出掩码扩散时间序列插补模型（MDTIM），通过掩码扩散的训练范式、MASK 标记与观测值的结构正交表示，以及随机离散化方法，在多个基准上一致超越了确定性和生成式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列插补因真实数据的复杂时间动态和噪声而极具挑战。现有方法存在两大局限：一是缺失值与观测值被嵌入同一表示空间，缺乏显式的结构分离；二是连续扩散方法被训练为预测加入的噪声，而非直接预测原始信号，学习目标与插补任务错位。

Method: 提出掩码扩散时间序列插补模型（MDTIM），借助掩码扩散模型的训练范式处理插补任务。具体包括：（1）引入与有效观测值结构正交的 MASK 标记，使模型直接预测原始值，令表示空间与学习目标自然对齐于插补任务；（2）提出随机离散化（Stochastic Discretization），将连续值映射为具有序数感知的离散标记，同时保持连续动态，从而弥合离散掩码扩散与时间序列连续、序数特性之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的实验表明，MDTIM 具有更强的鲁棒性和可扩展性，并在各类缺失场景下一致超越当前最先进的确定性与生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: MDTIM 通过结构正交的 MASK 表示、直接预测原始信号的目标，以及保持连续动态的随机离散化，有效解决了现有方法的表示空间不分离与学习目标错位问题，展现出优越的鲁棒性、可扩展性与插补性能。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [245] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFS+和PGFS++两种合成感知的强化学习框架，用于在保持可合成性和结构多样性的前提下改进分子性质（如成药性、结合亲和力）。PGFS+通过可训练的嵌入查找表与更优的评分函数和RL算法显著提升目标性质，但暴露出"奖励黑客"问题（反应物搜索将不同输入映射到同一高分分子、输出多样性崩溃）。PGFS++以输入分子为正向合成轨迹起点，利用学习到的反应模板和市售砌块生成兼具性质提升、明确合成路线和结构相似性的分子，实验表明其在提升性质的同时保持了高输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现常需改进分子性质，但在无约束化学空间中优化的分子若无法合成则实际价值有限。现有方法PGFS因通过反应物嵌入预测来间接选择反应物，限制了学习效果；而单纯增强反应物搜索又会导致奖励黑客（多样性崩溃），因此需要在保证可合成性与性质提升的同时，实现输入特异的分子改进并维持输出多样性。

Method: 作者首先提出PGFS+：将反应模板和第二反应物表示为可训练的嵌入查找表，并配合更有效的评分函数与强化学习算法直接优化性质。在此基础上，针对奖励黑客问题提出PGFS++：以输入分子作为正向合成轨迹的起点，逐步应用学习到的反应模板并选择兼容的市售（in-stock）砌块，生成改进性质的产物分子，同时保留明确的合成路线和与输入分子的结构相似性。

Result: 实验显示，PGFS+在提升目标性质上显著优于原PGFS，但也暴露了奖励黑客缺陷——强大的反应物搜索能将多样输入映射到同一高分"磁铁"分子，导致输出多样性崩塌。PGFS++通过输入特异的合成轨迹设计解决了该问题，在分子改进任务上既提升了目标性质，又保持了高输出多样性。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入训练嵌入查找表改进PGFS得PGFS+，并进一步提出合成感知、输入特异的PGFS++框架，成功解决了性质提升与输出多样性之间的张力。PGFS++在保证分子可合成性、提供明确合成路线、维持结构相似性的同时实现性质改进，为早期药物发现中的分子优化提供了更实用的强化学习方法。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [246] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出把梯度提升树(Gradient Boosting)集成的每棵树叶值当作坐标，将每个样本映射为R^M空间中的一个点（分数即各坐标之和），从而使对比解释完全精确，并据此构建了一个可审计、可执行的反事实建议(recourse)方法，在多个表格数据集上表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的可加性解释(SHAP等)对梯度提升树模型做了加性、采样或拟合假设，导致解释不精确且难以审计；作者希望找到一个空间（叶值坐标空间），使模型的线性结构明文可见，从而让反事实解释既精确又可追溯、可执行。

Method: 将每棵树的一个叶值重新解读为坐标而非中间结果，使每个实例成为R^M中的点，模型在点上呈线性作用（分数为坐标之和）。两个实例的差向量在共享叶子的坐标处严格为零，因此样本差异由少数可追踪到具体树、具体分裂的坐标承载。在此表示之上构建反事实建议方法，并引入“建议是否可被执行”的约束进行评测。

Result: 在五个表格数据集上重复交叉验证，推荐重构模型自身决策的误差达 6.2×10^-15，审计员无需模型即可复核算术；在信用数据集上，该方法在“努力-真实感”上为Pareto非支配；当建议被限制为被试实际可做的改变（排除年龄、已定谳的拖欠等）时，其有效性保留58%，而最强基线仅保留41%。

Conclusion: 将叶值视为坐标这一视角使梯度提升树的对比解释天然精确且可审计；基于此构建的反事实建议方法在精确重构、努力-真实感权衡以及“可执行性”约束下的有效性上均优于既有基线，并揭示标准评测因不检验建议是否可执行而掩盖了方法间的真实差距。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [247] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大脑信号到图像检索中低重复次数下准确率骤降的问题，发现存在"非传递性对齐"现象（低重复查询信号与图像表示各自与高重复中心对齐，但彼此不直接对齐），提出基于神经锚点的检索框架 NEAR，从查询去噪与候选图像伪锚点预测两侧向锚点靠拢，在 EEG/MEG/fMRI 四个数据集上显著提升少重复次数下的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑到图像检索通常需要对每个图像平均多达 80 次神经试验，重复刺激增加了延迟、成本与用户负担；当仅有一次或少数重复时检索准确率急剧下降。该下降通常被归因于查询噪声（平均可抑制噪声、提升信号稳定性），但作者发现仅用噪声无法完全解释，存在非传递性对齐模式，表明图库（gallery）放置方式也影响检索，因此需要新的框架解决少重复场景下的检索问题。

Method: 提出神经锚点检索框架 NEAR（neural-anchor-based retrieval）。核心思想是将高重复次数的中心视为"锚点"，并让查询侧与图库侧同时向锚靠拢：一个去噪器将低重复含噪查询拉向真实锚点；一个小型网络从候选图像预测其"伪锚点"并将该图像表示拉向伪锚点。通过在神经表示与视觉表示两侧共同锚定，缓解低重复查询信号与图像表示之间的对齐困难。

Result: 在涵盖 EEG、MEG 和 fMRI 的四个数据集上，NEAR 在少重复次数条件下一致提升检索性能。在 THINGS-EEG2 数据集上，平均 1 次与 4 次重复时，200 路 Top-1 准确率分别提升了 5.7 和 9.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过锚定神经表示与视觉表示，NEAR 减少了对重复采集的依赖，使神经检索更接近真实世界部署，证明图库放置与查询噪声共同影响少重复检索性能，而两侧向锚点靠拢的策略可有效改善这一场景。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [248] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无标签新用户，提出了一种无需目标标签即可恢复个体脑电坐标的框架 SCORE，在 200 路图像检索任务上显著优于现有基线，缩小了跨被试与同被试解码性能的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准视觉解码有神经通信等应用前景，但现有 EEG-to-image 检索方法对新用户（无标注校准数据）的表现远低于有校准的同被试方法，阻碍实际部署。作者分析跨被试 EEG 特征后发现：不同被试保持了相似的“概念间关系”，但沿着不同的坐标方向表达这些关系，导致直接的跨被试对齐失效。

Method: 提出 Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE) 框架，包含训练与部署两阶段：(1) 恢复感知源训练——在训练时将对齐源被试的 EEG 与公共图像空间，并通过“仅源被试 episode”模拟未见被试的坐标恢复过程；(2) 部署时坐标对齐——冻结双编码器，借助 hubness 修正的匹配选取可靠的 EEG-图像地标对，估计正交变换以恢复目标被试的 EEG 坐标。整个过程无需源数据、无需目标标签、不更新编码器。

Result: 在两个公开基准的 200 路检索任务上，SCORE 对每个目标被试均优于未自适应基线，并取得最佳总体准确率：在 THINGS-EEG2 上 Top-1/Top-5 达 53.23%/83.55%，在 Alljoined-1.6M 上达 12.01%/32.16%，分别比最强基线高出 17.45/15.70 和 3.08/4.62 个百分点。

Conclusion: 无需目标标签或编码器更新，SCORE 即可显著提升跨被试视觉解码性能，使基于脑电的视觉解码更接近稳健、实用、低延迟的跨用户部署。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [249] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"马尔可夫化间隙"（Markovization gap）这一概念，用于量化连续时间生成模型中"端点条件桥"被压缩为非预期性马尔可夫解码器时产生的不可约损失，并提出"桥图模型"（BGM）框架统一各类生成模型的设计选择，证明该间隙可作为训练前的设计诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型通常基于端点条件桥构建，但实际采样需要的是仅观测当前状态与时间的非预期性马尔可夫解码器。这种从桥到解码器的压缩是扩散模型、流匹配、整流流、薛定谔桥及场基生成模型共有的结构瓶颈，但此前缺乏统一的形式化工具来刻画和比较这一损失。

Method: 作者定义"马尔可夫化间隙"为给定马尔可夫状态时桥速度的条件方差的时间积分，等价于从采样器可用信息预测端点条件运动的MMSE（最小均方误差）。为跨模型族比较该瓶颈，提出"桥图模型"（BGM）形式化，将端点耦合、桥律、马尔可夫投影和保持流的动力学表示分离为独立设计选择；该形式化也将泊松模型与静电模型表示为具有对应场线马尔可夫化间隙的场线桥核。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的合成、潜空间和像素空间实验中，一个在训练前数分钟内估计的特征空间代理间隙，在固定架构、桥、采样器与计算预算的条件下，对设计选择的排序方向与下游训练损失和FID的排序方向一致。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫化间隙可作为桥与耦合设计的训练前诊断指标，有助于在昂贵训练之前识别和比较不同生成模型家族的设计优劣，弥补了此前缺乏统一跨族比较工具的空白。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [250] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过24次反事实实验（小规模训练32个124M参数GPT-2模型），直接测量单条训练样本对最终模型的影响。在训练早期注入一段文本，发现该文本会被一次性习得并在50步后仍可检测，但在训练结束时这种差异完全消失。注入仅将模型在其loss盆地内重新定位，而未将其移出盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究对单个训练样本对最终模型贡献的估计多依赖于影响函数等间接方法，误差较大；直接测量需要两次仅在单个batch的一行上不同的昂贵完整预训练，几乎无人做过。作者希望以可负担的小规模实验直接执行这一反事实测量，以揭示单样本影响的真实量级及其随训练的衰减规律。

Method: 在OpenWebText上从零训练32个124M参数的GPT-2模型，覆盖四个条件、八个种子。在9,536步中的第200步（学习率峰值处），将256行batch中的一行替换为固定的上下文注入（一段194 token的文本）。三个注入条件分别为：1) 具有语料库已证实主题的流畅散文；2) 流畅散文但其主题为虚构、且在全batch梯度delta上与前者匹配在0.14%以内；3) 随机键盘字符；第四个条件为未注入的双胞胎对照组。随后从交叉熵、插值损失屏障、逐层中心核对齐（CKA）、权重位移等几何指标，对学习后经历衰减的模型进行对比测量。

Result: 注入后50步，见过段落的模型分支比未见过者在该段落上的交叉熵低0.039和0.044 nats（8/8个种子，p<10^-4）。但到最终步，对两种段落均检测不到差异（p=0.25与p=0.71，最小可检测效应为0.025和0.079 nats），两段落之间亦无差异（p=0.54）。预注册的插值损失屏障对比为+0.0068（p=0.509，最小可检测效应0.032）。留出集交叉熵为-0.00044（p=0.310）。逐层CKA在任意层均无法区分任何条件。权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，并在训练中点已92%稳定，而屏障仅达种子间屏障的3.0%；两者相差约15倍，此为下界。

Conclusion: 单个训练样本可被一次性习得并短暂可检测，但其影响随训练充分衰减，到最终模型已不可测量。注入只在模型所在的损失盆地内重新定位模型，并未将其移出盆地。这为理解单样本贡献与模型参数的最终几何位置之间的关系提供了直接的反事实证据。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [251] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lévy Attention，一种针对不规则采样时间序列的交叉注意力算子，其输出是对非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分，能在单次确定性前向传播中以闭式形式同时给出预测及其可信度度量（证据量 Λ_q 与分歧量 tr Σ_V(q)），无需额外训练头部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对不规则采样时间序列的深度模型能在任意连续时间戳上回答问题，但无法报告每个答案应被信任的程度，即缺乏不确定性/可信度量化能力。

Method: 将注意力层的 softmax 替换为 Lévy Attention：query-key 兼容性在连续的（时间×通道）索引空间上组装出强度函数，非齐次泊松随机测度在该强度下散布原子，输出对这些原子处的插值值场求平均。期望上退化为磨光余弦核注意力，因此可替代 softmax 且具有精确梯度。利用精确方差恒等式，将证据量 Λ_q（总兼容质量）与分歧量 tr Σ_V(q)（值扩散）组合为 σ̂(q)=√(tr Σ_V(q)·φ(Λ_q))，作为采样算子的均方根偏差，由确定性前向传播直接输出。

Result: 分歧量携带主要信号，证据因子在稠密数据上无信息性、在稀疏数据上强信息性。在 t-PatchGNN 上，算子替换相对匹配对照组最多损失 5.6% 准确率，在最稀疏数据集上零损失。免费的分歧信号在五种子匹配套件上优于 20 次 MC dropout；σ̂ 缩放出校准的高斯分布，其零样本 CRPS 超过五十次抽样的采样器；split-conformal 包装在各置信水平达到标称覆盖率，单次前向在 1.4 秒内对 3,383 名未见患者按信任度排序。

Conclusion: Lévy Attention 通过随机积分形式，在不增加推理开销的前提下，把注意力层本身转化为同时输出预测与闭式可信度度量的算子，为不规则采样时间序列任务提供了高效、校准且带覆盖率保证的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD)，通过在长上下文任务中校准教师-验证器不一致问题，将验证器奖励与 token 级 OPD 信号结合，显著提升了 Qwen3-4B 和 8B 在五个长上下文基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文任务中，on-policy distillation (OPD) 的 token 级教师指导可能偏好局部合理但遗漏分散证据或违反全局任务约束的回答；而任务级验证器能返回反映部分成功的分级奖励。二者之间存在"教师-验证器不一致"，且随输入长度增加而加剧，因此在保留密集 token 级指导的同时纳入验证器结果成为核心动机。

Method: 作者提出 Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD)：在每个 rollout 组内分别对验证器奖励和轨迹级 OPD 分数做归一化，取其差值作为"带符号的教师-验证器不一致残差"；再通过 Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) 依据各 token 的相对 OPD advantage 将该轨迹级残差分配到 token，同时保留原始 OPD 信号。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，用 GC-OPD 后训练使官方 Qwen3-4B 的五个基准平均分从 29.08 提升到 40.47，Qwen3-8B 从 35.12 提升到 44.65；相同设置下 vanilla OPD 分别达到 39.31 和 43.56。控制消融表明带符号残差优于额外的 OPD 派生项或直接加归一化验证器奖励，RACA 进一步优于均匀 token 分配。

Conclusion: 组相对残差校准（group-relative residual calibration）能够在保留密集 token 级指导的同时有效纳入验证器结果，缓解长上下文任务中教师-验证器不一致的问题，并带来稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Using Spacing to Detect Multi-Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18228)
*Greg Kreider*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出通过对单变量数据的排序间隔（spacing）进行处理，利用其“U形+平坦底部”的特征来检测多模态性：多模态过渡区形成局部峰值以识别模态，平坦区定位众数，并开发了基于参数模型、游程/最长游程、置换/自助重采样以及结合变点检测器的多种特征检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量数据的间隔在单模态下呈“U”形（底部平坦、尾部快速上升），而多模态时各模态之间向尾部的过渡会在间隔序列中形成局部峰值特征。作者希望利用这些峰值特征来判断数据是否多模态，并定位各个众数。

Method: 对间隔进行处理并开发特征检验方法，包括：1）基于假设零分布的参数模型；2）对间隔符号差分中的游程数或最长游程进行检验；3）通过置换游程或基于信号差分的池（pool）进行自助重采样来重建特征；4）尝试结合现有的变点检测器来定位间隔行为发生变化的点。

Result: 文中给出了这些方法的完整处理流程、模型与检验的详细描述、若干使用示例，并总结了方法在一般情况下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过对排序间隔的峰值与平坦特征进行建模与检验，能够有效检测多模态性并定位众数，所提出的参数、游程、重采样及变点检测等综合方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: The spacing of a unimodal variate resembles a `U' with a flat bottom and rapidly increasing values in the tails. When combined into multi-modal setups, the transition between variates into their tails forms local increases in the spacing. These peak features signal the data is multi-modal while the flats locate the modes. We develop tests of the features, including parametric models based on an assumed null univariate distribution, the number of runs or longest run in the signed difference of the interval spacing, and feature reconstruction by means of permutations of the runs or bootstrap samples from a pool based on the difference of the signal. We also try combining existing changepoint detectors to look where the behavior of the spacing changes. This paper describes the processing of the spacing and models and tests in full, gives examples of their use, and summarizes the performance in general.

</details>


### [254] [Evaluating Spacing Tests of Multi-Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18243)
*Greg Kreider*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文基于数据间距（spacing）中的"平坦段（flats）小于峰值段、峰与峰之间增大"的特征，构建参数化、基于游程的非参数化及数据驱动的检验，用以识别数据中的多模态结构，并通过双模态设置与文献测试案例验证其分辨率、灵敏度、稳定性和零分布模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据点的间距特征蕴含模态信息：同一模态内间距一致（形成平坦段），不同模态之间间距增大（形成峰）。作者希望利用这一观察发展出判定多模态存在性及其位置的方法，并系统地检验这些检验的可靠性与适用范围。

Method: 定义了三种检验平坦段与峰值特征的检验方法：1）参数化检验；2）基于游程（runs）的非参数化检验；3）数据驱动的检验。通过两种情景进行评估：一是改变双模态设置以控制间距变化，考察检验的分辨率与灵敏度；二是将方法应用于文献中已有的大量模态研究测试案例，检验结果的稳定性与一致性；同时评估各特征零分布模型的准确性。

Result: 结果表明，数据间距确实能反映数据的模态结构；这些检验能够筛选出边缘（临界）情形；研究同时明确了分析开始失效的边界条件。

Conclusion: 数据间距所表现出的平坦段与峰值特征可有效用于多模态的检测与定位，所提出的多种检验在受控双模态情形和文献案例上均表现出一定效力，但存在适用的边界，超出该边界时分析将失效。

Abstract: The spacing of data contains information about the underlying modality. Sections of consistent, similar spacing correspond to modes while it increases between them. We have defined parametric, runs-based non-parametric, and data driven tests to evaluate these features --- flats and peaks --- and determine the presence and location of multiple modes. This report will check the tests in two situations. By varying a bi-modal setup we can control the changes in spacing and determine the resolution and sensitivity of the tests. By applying them to the large number of test cases that exist in the literature from other modality studies we check the stability and consistency of the results. We will also evaluate the accuracy of the null-distribution models of the features. The results show that the spacing does reflect the data's modality, that the tests do screen marginal cases, and where the analysis begins to break down.

</details>


### [255] [Debiased Inference for AI-Generated Data without Gold-Standard Labels: Identification via Multiple Imperfect Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18294)
*Naoki Egami,Sooahn Shin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出DMM框架，利用多个有误差的AI测量结果，在无需黄金标准标签的情况下实现有效的下游统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多学者使用AI测量变量并用于下游分析，但忽视AI预测误差会导致严重偏差和无效置信区间，即使准确率高达90%以上。现有的设计式监督学习和预测驱动推断等方法需要昂贵的黄金标准标签，在某些领域难以获取。

Method: 论文提出DMM（debiased inference with multiple imperfect measurements）框架，基于CP分解的成熟结果，假设多个有误差的AI测量在给定潜在真实标签和观测到的单元级特征（如文本嵌入）条件下相互独立。该框架允许未知误分类率在不同标注方法（如大语言模型）和不同标注单元（如文本）间变化。作者利用半参数推断理论证明DMM估计量具有一致性和渐近正态性。

Result: 仿真结果表明DMM能产生有效推断，且加入精确但不完美的测量可提升估计效率。针对大语言模型标注的常见应用，论文还开发了诊断方法来评估条件独立假设。

Conclusion: DMM框架通过结合多个有误差的AI测量，无需黄金标准标签即可实现有效的下游统计推断，为社会科学等领域的AI测量问题提供了更经济可行的解决方案。

Abstract: An increasing number of scholars use AI to measure variables they subsequently include in downstream analyses. Although AI-measured variables are often analyzed as if observed without error, ignoring prediction errors in automated measurement leads to substantial bias and invalid confidence intervals in downstream analyses, even if AI measurement accuracy is high, e.g., above 90%. Existing solutions, such as design-based supervised learning and prediction-powered inference, combine error-prone AI-based measurements with gold-standard labels, which may be costly and difficult to obtain in some application areas.
  In this paper, we propose debiased inference with multiple imperfect measurements (DMM), a framework that combines multiple error-prone AI measurements to enable valid downstream inference without gold-standard labels. Building on the established results on CP decomposition, DMM assumes that these measurements are independent conditional on the latent true label and observed unit-level features, such as text features represented by embeddings. This framework allows for unknown misclassification rates to vary across annotation methods (e.g., large language models) and across units of annotation (e.g., texts). Under this assumption, we use semiparametric inference theory to prove that the DMM estimator is consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling valid inference for a wide range of downstream statistical analyses common in the social sciences. Our simulation results show that DMM yields valid inference and that adding accurate, though imperfect, measurements can improve efficiency. Focusing on common applications of large language model annotations, we also develop diagnostics to assess the conditional independence assumption.

</details>


### [256] [Causal Mediation Analysis for an Interrupted Time Series: Stabilized Mediator Weighting with an Application to a Vehicle Emissions Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18326)
*Shalini Jayanetti,Sumeet Kalia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对单一中断时间序列（ITS）提出因果中介分析方法，通过稳定化中介加权估计自然直接与间接效应，并引入累积中介权重、第二中断事件和分块残差自助法，最终用于评估安大略省Drive Clean项目终止对地面臭氧的影响，发现直接效应显著而间接效应异质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群层面的政策在固定时点实施，通常仅通过单一汇总结果序列评估总效应。当政策预期通过可测量的中介路径发挥作用时，仅估计总效应信息量不足，需要将其分解为直接效应与间接效应，但中断时间序列设计下缺乏成熟的因果中介估计方法。

Method: 将单一中断时间序列形式化为因果中介模型，采用稳定化中介加权估计自然直接效应与自然间接效应。由于干预是确定性函数，暴露权重恒等于1，暴露对比通过分段回归的水平位移（level shift）识别，因此仅对中介路径加权。方法改进包括：引入累积中介权重以携带滞后混杂历史；纳入同期事件作为第二次中断；用分块残差自助法（移动分块重抽样中介与结局残差，保持确定性暴露时点不变）替代将估计权重视为固定的方差公式。

Result: 在按日数据校准的模拟中，未加权的系数乘积法在中介-结局混杂下存在偏倚，间接效应偏倚接近0.19、覆盖率仅0.003；稳定化加权将偏倚降至约0.03，并将间接效应覆盖率从近零提升至约0.83。应用于2019年安大略省Drive Clean车辆排放测试项目终止的实证中，方法估计地面臭氧直接减少2.113 ppb（95%区间-3.384至-0.841），在四个多伦多地区稳健；通过二氧化氮的间接效应为正但异质；总效应接近显著性边界；疫情前敏感性分析与主要结果一致。

Conclusion: 本文为单一中断时间序列提供了可行的因果中介估计框架，稳定化中介加权能有效校正中介-结局混杂偏倚并改善区间覆盖，成功将政策总效应分解为直接与间接效应，为政策机制评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: Population-level policies are introduced at a fixed time and evaluated from a single series of aggregate outcomes, and the interrupted time series design estimates the total shift in an outcome after the intervention. When the policy is expected to act through a measurable pathway, the total effect is less informative than its decomposition into direct and indirect effects. We formulate causal mediation for a single interrupted time series and study stabilized mediator weighting as the estimator of the natural direct and indirect effects. Because the intervention is a deterministic function, the exposure weight equals one and the exposure contrast is identified through the segmented-regression level shift, so only the mediator pathway is weighted. We add a cumulative mediator weight that carries the lagged confounder history, incorporate a concurrent event as a second interruption, and replace variance formulas that treat the estimated weights as fixed with a block-residual bootstrap that keeps the deterministic exposure timing intact and resamples the mediator and outcome residuals in moving blocks. In a simulation calibrated to daily data, the unweighted product-of-coefficients estimator is biased under mediator-outcome confounding, with an indirect-effect bias near $0.19$ and coverage of $0.003$, whereas stabilized weighting reduces the bias to about $0.03$ and improves indirect-effect coverage from near zero to about $0.83$. Applied to the 2019 termination of Ontario's Drive Clean vehicle emissions testing program, the method estimates a direct reduction in ground-level ozone of $2.113$ parts per billion (95\% interval $-3.384$ to $-0.841$) that is robust across four Toronto regions, a positive but heterogeneous indirect effect through nitrogen dioxide, and a total effect near the boundary of significance; a pre-pandemic sensitivity analysis agrees with the primary results.

</details>


### [257] [Centroid-Referenced Mahalanobis Matching (CRM): A Scalable, Representation-Based Framework for Causal Inference in Large Observational Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18417)
*Keming Hu,Yingpei He*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出质心参照马氏匹配（CRM），一种面向大规模因果推断的可扩展匹配方法。CRM 以两个参考坐标进行分层抽样替代全局成对搜索：每个单元到处理组质心的马氏距离，以及沿着处理-对照均值位移的 Fisher 坐标。其实现成本为 O(np²+p³+n log n)（n≥p 时简化为 O(np²+n log n)）。文中推导出分解表示、支撑、离散化和随机分量的误差界，并用预匹配短缺比例 π̂ 估计总体支撑限制，进而给出异质性处理效应有界时的差距界。实证上，CRM 在 Criteo 数据集上保留了至少 99.4% 的处理单元，MaxSMD 在 36 个大规模配置中的 31 个优于校正后的倾向得分匹配，速度快约一个数量级；中等规模模拟中，某些成对和加权基线在平衡性上更优，表明 CRM 的贡献在于可扩展性与显式支撑诊断，而非普遍的有限样本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模因果推断中的匹配方法计算代价高昂，且在重叠（overlap）有限时可能悄然改变目标总体。作者希望提出一种在计算上可扩展、并能显式监测与诊断支撑（support）问题的匹配方法。

Method: 提出质心参照马氏匹配（CRM）：以两个参考坐标——每个单元到处理组质心的马氏距离、以及沿处理-对照均值位移的 Fisher 坐标——进行分层抽样，替代全局成对搜索。所有协变量通过处理组协方差几何进入方法，因此不是"主成分预处理+最近邻匹配"。推导误差分解，分离表示、支撑、离散化与随机分量；用预匹配短缺比例 π̂ 估计人口支撑限制 π；容量驱动的排除与最终保留率被分开报告。

Result: 理论上，在表示充分性、平滑性与足够单元容量条件下，CRM 具有保守的二维直方图均方误差界 O(n_T^{-1/2})；表示充分性是额外假设，而非给定原始协变量可忽略性的推论。实证上，在 Criteo 数据集中 CRM 保留至少 99.4% 的处理单元，在 36 个大规模配置中的 31 个上 MaxSMD 低于校正后倾向得分匹配，且快约一个数量级；中等规模模拟中，部分成对和加权基线在平衡性上更优。

Conclusion: CRM 的核心贡献在于大规模下的可扩展性与显式支撑诊断，而非普遍的有限样本优势。其误差分解给出支撑限制 π 的差距界，并通过短缺比例与保留率分别报告支撑限制与容量排除，使匹配过程对目标总体的影响透明可监控。

Abstract: Matching for causal inference can be computationally expensive at scale and can silently change the target population when overlap is limited. We propose Centroid-Referenced Mahalanobis Matching (CRM), which replaces global pairwise search with stratified sampling in two reference coordinates: each unit's Mahalanobis distance from the treated centroid and its Fisher coordinate along the treated-control mean shift. All covariates enter through the treated covariance geometry; CRM is therefore not principal-component preprocessing followed by nearest-neighbor matching. For $n$ units and $p$ pretreatment covariates, its implemented cost is $O(np^2+p^3+n\log n)$, simplifying to $O(np^2+n\log n)$ when $n \ge p$.
  We derive an error decomposition separating representation, support, discretization, and stochastic components. A pre-matching shortage fraction $\hatπ$ estimates the population support restriction $π$, which enters a gap bound under bounded treatment-effect heterogeneity. Final retention is reported separately for capacity-driven exclusions. Under representation sufficiency, smoothness, and adequate cell capacity, CRM has a conservative two-dimensional histogram mean-squared-error bound $O(n_T^{-1/2})$; representation sufficiency is an additional assumption, not a consequence of ignorability given the original covariates.
  On Criteo, CRM retains at least 99.4% of treated units, has lower MaxSMD than corrected propensity-score matching in 31 of 36 large-scale configurations, and is roughly an order of magnitude faster. Moderate-size simulations favor some pairwise and weighting baselines on balance, locating CRM's contribution in scalability and explicit support diagnostics rather than universal finite-sample dominance.

</details>


### [258] [A seamless dose-optimization design for monotherapy and combination therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18435)
*Kentaro Takeda,Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无缝剂量优化设计，可在单一框架内同时自适应评估单药与联合治疗，基于疗效与毒性结局并使用患者回填策略，采用模型辅助的预定义贝叶斯最优边界，避免了实时模型拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 靶向药物与免疫肿瘤疗法的出现改变了肿瘤药物开发格局。传统针对细胞毒性药物的剂量探索方法假设单调的剂量-反应关系，但新型抗癌药常呈平台效应，需确定平衡疗效与耐受性的最佳生物剂量。FDA的Project Optimus强调通过平行随机队列和患者回填进行剂量优化。同时，当代开发越来越重视联合治疗，但现有设计通常假定两种药物角色对等，这与临床实践中新药联合剂量选项有限的既有药物的现实不符。

Method: 提出一种无缝剂量优化设计，通过自适应子试验并具备患者回填能力，同时评估单药与联合治疗。该模型辅助框架采用预定的贝叶斯最优边界，消除实时模型拟合，支持单药与联合治疗的同步评估，并支持带有策略性回填的顺序入组。

Result: 模拟研究显示，该设计在当代肿瘤学相关的多种剂量-反应模式下均表现出稳健性能。

Conclusion: 该设计弥合了方法学假设与临床现实之间的关键差距，提供了一种实用方法，将单药与联合治疗评估同基于疗效-毒性的回填策略相整合。

Abstract: The emergence of molecular-targeted agents and immune-oncology therapies has fundamentally transformed oncology drug development, necessitating evolution beyond traditional dose-finding approaches designed for cytotoxic agents. While conventional agents exhibit predictable monotonic dose-response relationships, novel anticancer agents often demonstrate plateau-effect patterns where higher doses may compromise therapeutic benefit, requiring identification of optimal biological doses that balance efficacy and tolerability. The FDA's Project Optimus initiative emphasizes comprehensive dose optimization through parallel randomized cohorts and patient backfilling to better understand pharmacological profiles across multiple dose levels. Contemporary drug development increasingly prioritizes combination therapy alongside monotherapy evaluation, yet existing designs typically assume equivalent roles for both agents, diverging from clinical practice where novel agents combine with established treatments having limited dose options. This paper proposes a seamless dose-optimization design that adaptively evaluates both monotherapy and combination therapy based on efficacy and toxicity outcomes through adaptive subtrials with patient backfilling capabilities. The model-assisted framework employs predetermined Bayesian optimal boundaries, eliminating real-time model fitting while accommodating evaluation of both monotherapy and combination therapy and enabling sequential enrollment with strategic backfilling. Simulation studies demonstrate robust performance across diverse dose-response patterns relevant to contemporary oncology. The design addresses critical gaps between methodological assumptions and clinical reality, offering a practical approach that integrates monotherapy and combination therapy evaluation with efficacy-toxicity-based backfilling.

</details>


### [259] [gridcp: Fast Online Changepoint Detection in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18695)
*Per August Jarval Moen,Sebastian Grau Nielsen,Espen Bjørge Urheim,Martin Tveten,Ingrid Kristine Glad*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的 Python 包 gridcp，它基于网格化离线检验统计量方法，将各类离线变点检测转化为高效在线检测器，提供统一接口、多种内置检验、阈值校准（假警报率/平均运行长度）与数据驱动校准，并通过模拟和真实数据验证了准确性、可扩展性与低检测延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线变点检测存在计算瓶颈：离线方法直接用于流式数据时，每个观测的计算成本与内存通常随样本量线性增长，无法实时运行。Moen (2026) 提出的网格化方法通过在稀疏几何网格上评估离线检验统计量，使更新时间和内存随流长度对数增长，并给出有限样本检测延迟保证。本文旨在将该方法落地为易用、统一且高效的开源软件工具。

Method: 构建 gridcp Python 包：在稀疏几何网格（越往过去网格点间隔越大）上评估离线检验统计量；提供九个 Numba 加速的内置检验（覆盖均值、方差、协方差、回归系数变化，非参数检验及指数族模型的广义似然比检验），并支持用户自定义检验；通过 Monte Carlo 例程将检测阈值校准到目标假警报概率或平均运行长度，无参数零模型时提供数据驱动变体。

Result: 校准精度高；运行时随流长度和维度扩展良好（对数/受控增长）；从校准到部署的完整流水线在长真实数据流上运行高效，且检测延迟短。三个真实数据案例研究验证了上述结论。

Conclusion: gridcp 成功将网格化离线检验方法转化为统一、高效的在线变点检测软件包，涵盖多种检验类型并支持自定义，其阈值校准准确、扩展性好、部署高效，为实时变点检测提供了实用的开源工具。

Abstract: Online changepoint detection is the problem of detecting distributional changes in a data stream in real-time. A large body of methodology exists for the offline (fixed-size) setting, but applying these methods online quickly becomes infeasible since the per-observation computational cost and memory consumption typically grow at least linearly with the sample size. A recently proposed grid-based methodology (Moen, 2026) overcomes this by evaluating an offline test statistic over a sparse geometric grid of split points, with grid points spaced increasingly far apart further in the past. For a wide class of test statistics, this approach keeps update time and memory consumption growing logarithmic in the length of the data stream, while admitting finite-sample guarantees on the detection delay. Building on this methodology, we present gridcp, an open-source Python package that turns offline changepoint tests into efficient online detectors through a single, uniform interface. Users can choose from nine Numba-accelerated built-in tests, spanning changes in the mean, variance, covariance, and regression coefficients, as well as nonparametric tests and generalized likelihood-ratio tests for exponential-family models. Users can also supply their own test, which the package handles identically. For any test, gridcp provides Monte Carlo routines that calibrate the detection threshold to a target false alarm probability or average run length, including data-driven variants when no parametric null model is available. Through simulations and three real-data case studies, we show that calibration is accurate, that runtime scales favorably with both stream length and dimension, and that the complete pipeline, from calibration to deployment, runs efficiently on long real-world streams with only a short detection delay.

</details>


### [260] [Robust Modeling of Extremes in the Presence of Inliers with Enhanced Tail Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18735)
*Shivshankar Nila,Ishapathik Das,N. Balakrishna*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稳健的极值建模框架，能够同时处理内点（inliers，如瞬时/早期失效）、极端值和尾部比例，以解决极值理论中阈值选择的经典难题，并通过最大似然估计参数。理论与模拟及实证表明，该方法在存在内点时比经典诊断工具和现有极值混合模型具有更准确的阈值估计与尾部推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极值理论是稀有和极端事件建模的基础框架，但阈值选择一直是个难题，尤其当数据中存在内点（如瞬时或早期失效）时。在可靠性研究和环境数据等应用中，靠近原点或位于原点的观测簇会严重扭曲经典阈值选择程序和尾部推断，因此需要一种能同时容纳内点、极端值和尾部比例的稳健建模方法。

Method: 提出一个包含内点、极端值和尾部比例的稳健建模框架，采用最大似然法进行参数估计。将其与经典数值/图形诊断工具（平均超出量图、参数稳定性图、Hill图和Pickands图）以及现有极值混合模型进行对比，并建立模型的理论性质，通过大量蒙特卡洛模拟和真实数据应用评估其性能。

Result: 模拟与实证结果表明，在存在内点的情况下，所提方法相比现有经典方法提供了更准确的阈值估计和更可靠的极值推断，能够有效克服现有方法的关键局限。

Conclusion: 所提方法在存在内点时能提供更准确的阈值估计和尾部推断，弥补了现有方法的不足，为可靠性、环境数据等应用中受内点干扰的极值分析提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Extreme value theory provides a fundamental framework for modeling rare and extreme events; however, threshold selection remains a persistent challenge, particularly in the presence of inliers such as instantaneous or early failures. Such observations commonly arise in applications including reliability studies and environmental data, where clusters of observations near the origin or at the origin can substantially distort classical threshold selection procedures and tail inference. In this paper, we propose a robust modeling framework that accounts for inliers, extremes, and the tail proportion. Parameter estimation is carried out using maximum likelihood. The proposed methodology is compared with classical numerical and graphical diagnostic tools, including the mean excess plot, parameter stability plot, Hill plot, and Pickands plot, as well as existing extreme value mixture models. The theoretical properties of the proposed model are established, and its performance is evaluated through extensive Monte Carlo simulations and real-data applications. The results demonstrate that the proposed methodology provides more accurate threshold estimation and more reliable extreme-value inference in the presence of inliers compared with existing classical approaches. Overall, the proposed methodology provides more accurate threshold estimation and tail inference in the presence of inliers, addressing key limitations of existing methods.

</details>


### [261] [Regularised Iterative Generalised Least Squares with Optimal Selection of the Hyper-Parameter for Identifying Nonlinear Phenomenological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18742)
*Mark Cary,Charles Bokor*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对锂离子电池健康状态（SoH）预测等经验主导领域中，基于准物理思想的唯象模型存在参数完全或部分混杂、难以可靠估计的问题，提出一种岭回归方案，并给出基于信息论性能度量的自动超参数优化方法，结合正则化迭代广义最小二乘，可拟合异方差和序列相关数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在锂离子电池SoH预测等领域，唯象模型虽具准物理意义，但其参数常因模型结构而完全或部分混杂，导致数值病态、参数难以可靠估计；研究者希望在保留模型形式的同时改善数值条件。

Method: 引入岭回归（ridge regression）方案；基于信息论模型性能度量自动优化岭回归超参数（每次迭代需用不动点迭代求解）；给定合适初值时收敛极快。将该超参数选择机制嵌入正则化迭代广义最小二乘（regularised iterative generalised least squares）框架，以支持异方差和序列相关数据的拟合。

Result: 分析表明超参数的不动点迭代收敛非常迅速；仿真实验验证了整体方法（自动超参数选择+正则化迭代广义最小二乘）的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提岭回归与自动超参数选择机制能在保持唯象模型形式的同时改善参数估计的数值条件，并适用于异方差和序列相关数据，整体方法经仿真验证有效。

Abstract: In some fields currently dominated by empirical approaches, such as state of health (SoH) prediction for lithium-ion batteries, phenomenological models motivated by quasi-physical thinking contain parameters to be estimated from experimental data. Often the structure of such models yields fully or partially confounded parameters, which are difficult or even impossible to estimate reliably. To preserve the desired model formulation and simultaneously improve the numerical conditioning for the problem we introduce a ridge regression scheme. An automated method is provided, based on information theoretic measures of model performance, which optimises the ridge regression hyper-parameter at each iteration. The formulae presented require fixed point iteration to solve for the hyper-parameter. Given a suitable starting value, analysis demonstrates convergence is very rapid. The optimal hyper-parameter selection mechanism is incorporated within an efficient regularised iterative generalised least squares mechanism, capable of fitting both heteroscedastic and serially correlated data as required. Simulation confirms the efficacy of the overall method.

</details>


### [262] [Functional forms in joint models for longitudinal and time-to-event data: A practical guide with application and interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18858)
*Felix Boakye Oppong,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Thierry Gorlia,Nicole Erler*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了联合模型中将纵向生物标志物轨迹与生存事件风险关联起来的不同函数形式（function form），并强调这一选择直接决定 biomarker-风险关系的解释。作者通过 MIRAGE 胶质母细胞瘤试验数据（纵向白细胞计数与总生存期）展示，瞬时效应、累积/变化型、共享随机效应和变异性结构各自捕捉轨迹的不同特征，参数不可直接比较，甚至可能导致相反结论，因此函数形式的选择应密切贴合科学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型在临床研究中应用广泛，但纵向轨迹与事件风险之间函数形式（association structure）的选择常被当作技术细节处理，缺乏系统关注。默认设定可能遗漏临床上重要的轨迹特征，导致模型假设失当、效应解释失真，因此有必要对各类函数形式进行结构化比较并阐明其对结论的影响。

Method: 作者提供一个结构化综述（structured overview），系统比较多种关联结构：瞬时效应（当前值 current value、斜率 slope、加速度 acceleration）、累积型（cumulative）与基于变化的（change-based）公式、共享随机效应（shared random effects）以及基于变异性（variability-based）的关联。并利用 MIRAGE 胶质母细胞瘤试验中纵向白细胞计数与总生存期数据进行实证演示，考察不同函数形式在生物标志物量尺和时间量尺上的表现及所刻画的 biomarker-风险关系。

Result: 瞬时形式捕捉生物标志物的当前水平或短期动态；累积型和变化型反映长期暴露或趋势；变异性结构将轨迹不稳定性量化为一种替代性的预后信号。关联参数依赖于所选的函数形式、生物标志物量尺和时间量尺，因此不同设定下的效应量不可直接比较。在 MIRAGE 应用中，不同函数形式产生了不同的效应解释，部分情形下甚至对 biomarker-风险关系得出了不同结论。

Conclusion: 函数形式的选择是联合模型中的一个关键建模决策，决定了 biomarker-风险关联的解释。为保证解释有效并实现透明报告，所选函数形式必须与具体的科学问题对齐。

Abstract: Background: Joint models for longitudinal and time-to-event data are widely used in clinical research. However, the choice of functional form linking the biomarker trajectory to event risk is often treated as a technical detail, despite its importance for model assumptions and interpretation. Default specifications may fail to capture clinically relevant features of biomarker trajectories.
  Methods: We provide a structured overview of functional forms linking longitudinal and survival processes in joint models. We compare association structures including instantaneous effects (current value, slope, and acceleration), cumulative and change-based formulations, shared random effects, and variability-based associations. Using longitudinal white blood cell measurements and overall survival data from the MIRAGE glioblastoma trial, we illustrate how different functional forms capture distinct features of biomarker trajectories and define different biomarker-risk relationships.
  Results: Instantaneous forms capture the biomarker's current level or short-term dynamics, whereas cumulative and change-based forms reflect longer-term exposure or trends. Variability-based structures quantify instability in the biomarker trajectory as an alternative prognostic signal. Association parameters depend on the functional form, biomarker scale, and time scale, and effect sizes are therefore not directly comparable. In the MIRAGE application, alternative functional forms produced different effect interpretations and, in some cases, different conclusions regarding the biomarker-risk relationship.
  Conclusions: The choice of functional form is a key modelling decision in joint models and determines the interpretation of the biomarker-risk association. Aligning the functional form with the scientific question is essential for valid interpretation and transparent reporting.

</details>


### [263] [Model-based bootstrap inference for Cox models after Lasso selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18893)
*Lena Schemet,Andreas Groll,Sarah Friedrich-Welz*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的bootstrap方法，用于Cox-Lasso变量选择后的统计推断。方法先对原始数据拟合Cox-Lasso选定变量，再对所选变量拟合无惩罚Cox模型，并基于此半参数plug-in模型生成bootstrap样本，在每个bootstrap样本中固定所选变量、仅重新拟合无惩罚Cox模型，从而改善选择后条件覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cox回归中变量选择后的推断具有挑战性：简单的Wald型区间在选定变量后有限样本条件覆盖率较差，需要一种能提供有效不确定性量化的推断方法。

Method: 先对原始数据拟合Cox-Lasso选定变量集合，再用该变量集合拟合无惩罚Cox模型；由该无惩罚系数估计、Breslow基线累积风险估计及plug-in删失分布构建半参数plug-in Cox模型并生成bootstrap样本；每个bootstrap样本中固定所选变量，仅重新拟合无惩罚Cox模型。在oracle型稀疏模型假设与标准Cox模型正则条件下证明一阶bootstrap有效性，并考察百分位数与studentized bootstrap区间。

Result: 模拟中百分位数与studentized bootstrap区间在多个小样本与中等样本场景下，相对bootstrap-Wald区间改善了条件覆盖率；其表现与去偏（debiased）区间大体相当，但对比结果取决于信号强度、调参及选择稳定性。SEER乳腺癌实例表明该方法可在真实生存分析中实现，并对选择后报告的效应提供可解释的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该基于模型的bootstrap方法为一阶有效的Cox-Lasso选择后推断提供了可行方案，改进了有限样本条件覆盖率，适用于真实生存分析场景。

Abstract: Inference after variable selection in Cox regression is difficult because simple Wald-type intervals after selection can have poor finite-sample conditional coverage. We study a model-based bootstrap for inference after Cox-Lasso variable selection. The Cox-Lasso is fitted once to the original data to select a set of variables, after which an unpenalized Cox model is fitted using only those variables. Bootstrap samples are generated from a semiparametric plug-in Cox model specified by the coefficient estimate from this unpenalized Cox refit, the Breslow baseline cumulative hazard estimator, and a plug-in censoring distribution. In every bootstrap sample, the selected variable set is kept fixed and only the unpenalized Cox model is refitted. Under oracle-type sparse-model assumptions and standard Cox model regularity conditions, we prove first-order bootstrap validity for this procedure. In the simulation scenarios considered, percentile and studentized bootstrap intervals showed improved conditional coverage relative to the bootstrap-Wald interval in several small- and moderate-sample settings. Their performance was broadly competitive with debiased intervals, although the comparison depended on signal strength, tuning, and selection stability. A SEER breast cancer example illustrates that the procedure can be implemented in a realistic survival analysis and provides interpretable uncertainty quantification for effects reported after variable selection.

</details>


### [264] [Mixed Membership Model of Low-rank Matrices with Multimodal Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18953)
*David Snider,Zhongyuan Lyu,Jian Kang,Yuqi Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对矩阵型数据的低秩混合成员模型，将每个受试者矩阵的期望表示为若干潜在低秩基矩阵的凸组合，兼顾可解释的总体原型与连续的受试者隶属度，并给出可辨识性条件、估计器与算法、非渐近误差界及在HCP功能连接数据上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵型观测数据（如多重网络、神经影像）中总体模式常为低秩，且受试者可能同时表达多个潜在模式。现有张量PCA方法给出连续得分但载荷矩阵难以解释为总体原型；低秩聚类虽给出可解释原型却只能得到硬标签。作者希望同时获得可解释的总体极端剖面和连续的受试者级隶属度，并解决单一模态下可辨识性不足的问题。

Method: 提出低秩混合成员模型：期望矩阵为潜在低秩基矩阵的凸组合；在纯受试者条件下证明可辨识性；提出带约束的最小二乘估计器及可扩展算法（谱初始化加低秩精化）；扩展为多模态版本以跨模态共享隶属度、各模态拥有独立基矩阵；推导非渐近误差界。

Result: 估计器达到对数因子内的极小极大最优重构率，并在几何条件下给出基矩阵与隶属度各自的收敛率；模拟验证了理论速率并表现出强性能；在人类连接组计划功能连接数据上识别出可解释的脑连接剖面，其估计隶属度与认知表型显著相关。

Conclusion: 所提低秩混合成员模型和方法能同时提供可解释的总体剖面与连续隶属度，理论性质（可辨识性、最优重构率、分项收敛率）经模拟验证，并在真实神经影像数据上得到可解释且与认知表型关联的脑连接模式，验证了方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Matrix-valued observations arise in multiplex networks, neuroimaging, and other domains where population-level patterns are often low-rank and subjects may express several latent patterns simultaneously. Existing tensor PCA methods provide continuous subject scores but their loading matrices can be difficult to interpret as population prototypes, while low-rank clustering yields interpretable prototypes with hard labels. We introduce a low-rank mixed membership model for matrix-valued data in which the expected value of each subject's matrix is a convex combination of latent low-rank basis matrices. The model yields both interpretable population-level extreme profiles and continuous subject-level memberships. Our multimodal extension shares memberships across modalities with modality-specific basis matrices and can restore identifiability when one modality is insufficient. We establish identifiability under a pure-subject condition, propose a constrained least-squares estimator and scalable algorithm with spectral initialization and low-rank refinement, and derive nonasymptotic error bounds. The estimator achieves a minimax-optimal reconstruction rate up to a logarithmic factor, with separate basis and membership convergence rates under a geometric condition. Simulations corroborate the theoretical rates and show strong performance. In an analysis of Human Connectome Project functional connectivity data, the proposed method identifies interpretable brain connectivity profiles whose estimated memberships are strongly associated with cognitive phenotypes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
